Reads project_proposal.json\n2. Extracts template.hwpx\n3. Replaces placeholders in XML files\n4. Computes month spans for phase lines\n5. Normalizes budget (removes commas after currency symbol)\n6. Removes stale layout caches from modified paragraphs\n7. Repackages into final HWPX\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport zipfile\nfrom xml.etree import ElementTree as ET\n\n# Paths\nROOT_DIR = \"/root\"\nWORK_DIR = \"/tmp/hwpx_work\"\nTEMPLATE_HWPX = os.path.join(WORK_DIR, \"template.hwpx\")\nEXTRACT_DIR = os.path.join(WORK_DIR, \"extracted\")\nOUTPUT_HWPX = os.path.join(ROOT_DIR, \"project_proposal_ready.hwpx\")\nJSON_PATH = os.path.join(ROOT_DIR, \"project_proposal.json\")\n\n# Namespace\nHWP_NS = \"http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML\"\nNSMAP = {\"hp\": HWP_NS}\n\n\ndef parse_date(date_str):\n    \"\"\"Parse date string like '2026-08' or '2026-08-01' to (year, month)\"\"\"\n    parts = date_str.split('-')\n    year = int(parts[0])\n    month = int(parts[1])\n    return year, month\n\n\ndef calculate_month_span(date_range_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate month span from date range string.\n    Handles formats like:\n    - '2026-08 ~ 2026-10'\n    - '(2026-08 ~ 2026-10)'\n    - '2027-01' (single month)\n    Returns the number of months (inclusive).\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove parentheses\n    clean = date_range_str.strip().strip('()')\n\n    if '~' in clean:\n        start_str, end_str = clean.split('~', 1)\n        start_str = start_str.strip()\n        end_str = end_str.strip()\n    else:\n        # Single month\n        start_str = end_str = clean.strip()\n\n    try:\n        start_year, start_month = parse_date(start_str)\n        end_year, end_month = parse_date(end_str)\n\n        # Calculate inclusive months\n        months = (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n        return months\n    except (ValueError, IndexError):\n        return None\n\n\ndef normalize_budget(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize budget: remove commas from numeric part but keep currency symbol.\n    Example: '₩450,000,000' -> '₩450000000'\n    \"\"\"\n    # Match currency symbol followed by number with commas\n    match = re.match(r'^([₩$€£¥])?([\\d,]+)$', value.strip())\n    if match:\n        currency = match.group(1) or ''\n        number = match.group(2).replace(',', '')\n        return currency + number\n    return value\n\n\ndef get_paragraph_text(p_elem):\n    \"\"\"Get all text content from a paragraph element.\"\"\"\n    text_parts = []\n    for t_elem in p_elem.iter():\n        if t_elem.tag == f'{{{HWP_NS}}}t':\n            text_parts.append(t_elem.text or '')\n    return ''.join(text_parts)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text(p_elem, new_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Set text content of a paragraph, handling multiple <hp:t> elements.\n    Returns True if modified, False otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Find all <hp:run> elements with <hp:t> children\n    runs_with_t = []\n    for run in p_elem.findall(f'{{{HWP_NS}}}run'):\n        t_elems = list(run.findall(f'{{{HWP_NS}}}t'))\n        if t_elems:\n            runs_with_t.append((run, t_elems))\n\n    if not runs_with_t:\n        return False\n\n    # Put all text in the first <hp:t>, remove subsequent <hp:t> elements\n    first_run, first_t_list = runs_with_t[0]\n    if first_t_list:\n        first_t_list[0].text = new_text\n        # Remove extra <hp:t> elements from first run\n        for t_elem in first_t_list[1:]:\n            first_run.remove(t_elem)\n\n    # Remove <hp:t> elements from subsequent runs (and empty runs if any)\n    for run, t_list in runs_with_t[1:]:\n        for t_elem in t_list:\n            run.remove(t_elem)\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarray(p_elem):\n    \"\"\"Remove all <hp:linesegarray> child elements from paragraph.\"\"\"\n    for elem in list(p_elem):\n        if elem.tag == f'{{{HWP_NS}}}linesegarray':\n            p_elem.remove(elem)\n\n\ndef process_paragraph(p_elem, replacements):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a paragraph: replace placeholders, compute month spans, remove layout cache.\n    Returns True if paragraph was modified.\n    \"\"\"\n    original_text = get_paragraph_text(p_elem)\n    modified_text = original_text\n    was_modified = False\n\n    #
